(3) **Drag gesture moved off the whole view** onto just the artwork area — previously the gesture competed with the Slider's drag gesture in the controls section, possibly preventing dismissal. Also added `.minimumScaleFactor(0.7)` to title/artist so long names shrink rather than truncate.
- **2026-06-06** — Player UX fixes from on-device feedback: (1) **MiniPlayerBar slimmed** — 66pt → ~52pt by reducing vertical padding (12→8) and artwork (42→36). Removed the dismiss-X (unusual for music mini-players; deviated from Apple Music convention and ate space). Replaced solid `primary700` background with `.ultraThinMaterial` + 72%-opacity colour overlay so the bar reads as floating glass rather than a third tab bar. Added a 0.5pt top hairline so the bar still feels like its own surface. (2) **NowPlayingView dismissibility fixed** — old version applied `.ignoresSafeArea()` to the *entire* view, so the close button rendered under the status bar / Dynamic Island and was invisible. Now only the blurred background ignores safe area; control VStack respects it. Three dismiss paths: (a) bigger 44×44 chevron-down button with a translucent black circle for visibility against any artwork, (b) tappable drag handle at top-center, (c) **swipe-down gesture** with offset animation + 120pt threshold + spring snapback — matches Apple Music's standard pattern.
- **2026-06-06** — **Per-emoji Spline scene routing.** Refactored from "one demo snapshot" to "per-emoji scene URL → per-URL snapshot." Single source of truth is `Spline.perEmojiSceneURLs` in `Spline3DAvatarView.swift` — a `[String: URL]` mapping every entry in `userAvatarEmojis` to a Spline scene. `Spline.sceneURL(for emoji: String?) -> URL` is the lookup; falls back to `demoSceneURL` for unknown emojis. **Today every entry points at the demo URL** — the per-emoji infrastructure is plumbed, but the 24 designed animal-character scenes don't exist yet. To go fully live, replace the right-hand side of `perEmojiSceneURLs` with real scene URLs; every avatar in the app picks up the new scenes with zero other code changes. `SplineSnapshotCache` rewritten from `demoSnapshot: UIImage?` to `cache: [URL: UIImage]` keyed by scene URL — `warm(sceneURL:size:)` and `snapshot(for:)` are the public API. `NearMeCanvasView.warmSnapshotsForVisibleUsers()` walks the visible users, collects distinct scene URLs, warms each (in-flight + cached URLs are skipped); re-warms when the visible set changes (radius bump, new arrivals). NearMeUserSheet uses `Spline.sceneURL(for: user.avatarEmoji)` so once real scenes exist, each user's sheet shows their own animal.
- **2026-06-06** — **NearMe canvas now 3D via snapshot pipeline.** New `SplineSnapshotCache` (`Nodalong/Shared/UI/`) renders the Spline scene to a `UIImage` once off-screen, then every avatar on the canvas displays the cached bitmap. Solves the "N live Spline scenes is too expensive" performance problem — one Metal render context for the warm, zero per-avatar after. How it works: mounts a `SplineView` in a hidden offscreen `UIHostingController` (in a `UIWindow` at negative coordinates — detached hosting controllers don't paint), waits for `SplineViewPhase.success` via a probe view, gives Metal 0.5s to actually paint, snapshots the host's view via `UIGraphicsImageRenderer.drawHierarchy(in:afterScreenUpdates: false)` (NOT SwiftUI's `ImageRenderer` — that doesn't capture Metal content reliably), tears the host down. `NearMeCanvasView` observes the singleton and switches each `avatarLayer` to the cached bitmap once it's ready; falls back to 2D `AvatarView` while loading and on simulator. Each avatar gets a 3pt ring in the user's accent colour so they're still individuated even though every snapshot is the same demo character (per-scene granularity comes once custom scenes exist). Cache warms on first `NearMeView.onAppear`.
- **2026-06-06** — **Spline iOS Runtime SDK integrated.** Added `https://github.com/splinetool/spline-ios` (`SplineRuntime` product) via `project.yml`. New `Spline3DAvatarView` wrapper renders a live `SplineView(sceneFileURL:)`; wired into `NearMeUserSheet`'s header. **Critical gotcha:** Spline's binary `.xcframework` does NOT include an iOS Simulator slice — first device build succeeds, first simulator build fails with `unsupported Swift architecture`. Guard the import + the view body with `#if !targetEnvironment(simulator)` and provide an `AvatarView` fallback for simulator builds. Don't remove the guards. Currently using Spline's public demo scene URL (`build.spline.design/nDkzGcy787CUJYaEI-gy`) as a placeholder until custom animal-character scenes are produced — see `Spline.demoSceneURL` in `Spline3DAvatarView.swift`. NearMe canvas still uses the 2D `AvatarView` (the planned snapshot-cache approach for multi-avatar canvases hasn't landed yet).
- **2026-06-06** — **Avatar 3D-feel + asset-pipeline ready.** `AvatarView` now has two render paths and an animation flag. Render: looks up `Assets.xcassets` for an image named `avatar_<key>` (key derives from emoji via `assetMapping`); if present, renders the PNG (intended target: pre-rendered 3D character art). Otherwise falls back to the raw emoji character. Animation: new `animated: Bool` param (default `false`). When `true`, applies depth styling (radial highlight upper-left, shadow gradient lower-right, colour-tinted drop shadow) + idle bob/tilt (~8° rotation3DEffect + 4% Y offset, randomised start delay per instance so a canvas full of avatars doesn't move in lockstep). **Only the NearMe canvas sets `animated: true`** — Profile, TrackCard attribution, search rows, follow lists, sheets all stay flat. The asset names are documented in the mapping table at the bottom of `AvatarView.swift` — drop PNGs into `Resources/Assets.xcassets` matching those names and they appear automatically; no code change.
- **2026-06-06** — **Avatar system replaced.** Old pixel-sprite `CharacterSpriteView` (with `characterType: Int` + 4 hand-drawn sprite shapes + animated wandering/listening/bumping states) is gone. New system: **`AvatarView` renders an emoji on a coloured circle.** Two axes only — pick an animal emoji from the curated `userAvatarEmojis` set (24 animals) + a background colour from `userAccentColors`. Native iOS emoji font, zero external dependencies, scales perfectly at any size. Schema: added `avatarEmoji: String?` to `UserProfile` + `Post` (Firestore field `avatarEmoji`), kept `avatarCharacter: Int?` as a tombstone for legacy decoding only (UI never reads it). New `AvatarPickerView` is two `LazyVGrid`s with spring-animated selection. Replaced at every call site: `ProfileView`, `UserProfileView`, `NearMeCanvasView`, `NearMeUserSheet`, `SearchView` user rows, `RegisterView`, `EditProfileView`. `FirebaseAuthViewModel.register/updateProfile` now take `avatarEmoji`. NearMe canvas avatars get a subtle `.scaleEffect(1.15)` on `.bumping` to preserve the playful physics feel without sprite animation. Deleted: `CharacterSpriteView.swift`, `Phase2Preview.swift`. Moved `AvatarAnimState` enum into `AvatarAnimationState.swift` where it belongs.
- **2026-06-06** — Profile recent-tracks improvements: (1) `AppleMusicService.getRecentlyPlayed` now sets `req.limit = 25` — default was ~10, which was the bottleneck on how many recents reached Firestore. (2) Fixed two stale `.spotify` refs in `Posts.swift` from the original port: `syncRecentTracks` was writing `"sourceService": "spotify"` for every Apple Music sync, and `mapRecentTrack` was hardcoding `.spotify` on every read. Read path now decodes the persisted value when present and defaults to `.appleMusic` for older docs. (3) New shared `RecentTracksCarousel` component — pages of 10 `TrackCard`s, horizontal swipe between pages, custom dot indicator (default SwiftUI `.page` dots are invisible on our dark background). Used by both `ProfileView` (full callbacks — own can spotlight) and `UserProfileView` (read-only spotlight — pass `onSpotlight: nil`). Fixes the inconsistency where own profile showed cards and other profiles showed plain HStack rows.
- **2026-06-06** — MusicKit token issue fully resolved on device; search + playback working. Added defensive empty-`MusicItemID` guards in `AppleMusicService`: `toSong` / `toArtistResult` / `toAlbumResult` now return `Optional` and `nil` for records with empty identifiers; all call sites switched from `.map` to `.compactMap`. Also early-returns from `getArtistAlbums` / `getAlbumTracks` if the input id is empty. Addresses MusicKit's internal `MPIdentifierSet EMPTY` log noise — items with no identifier never reach the UI now.
- **2026-06-06** — Correction to the entitlements step above: **MusicKit does NOT need an entry in the entitlements file.** Xcode signing returned `Entitlement com.apple.developer.musickit not found and could not be included in profile. This likely is not a valid entitlement and should be removed from your entitlements file.` MusicKit is gated entirely by (1) ticking MusicKit under **App Services** on the App ID at developer.apple.com and (2) `NSAppleMusicUsageDescription` in Info.plist. The token service then authorises by bundle ID — no binary-level entitlement is involved. Removed the `entitlements:` block from `project.yml` and deleted `Nodalong/Nodalong.entitlements`. **Don't re-add it.**
- **2026-06-06** — Root-caused "search returns nothing" + "track unavailable" via diagnostic logging: `developerTokenRequestFailed` from Apple's Media API Token Service ("client not found" — bundle ID `com.bigdeccyh.nodalong` wasn't registered as a MusicKit client). User enabled MusicKit under **App Services** for the App ID at developer.apple.com. That's the real fix. Library reads worked the whole time because they use a different token path.
- **2026-06-06** — Bug fixes from on-device testing: (1) NearMe radius slider moved from `.toolbar(.bottomBar)` to a top control row inside the content — was hidden behind tab bar + MiniPlayerBar. (2) Duration display fixed: stored in **ms** (legacy Spotify schema), display sites were treating it as seconds. Added shared `formatTrackDuration(_ ms: Int)` in NodalongKit/Utils and replaced local helpers in ShelfView + AlbumDetailSearchView. (3) Replaced silent `try?` in PlayerViewModel.play / resolve and SearchViewModel.fetchMusic with `do/catch + print("[Nodalong.X] …")` so MusicKit errors surface in the Xcode console — diagnoses the "track unavailable" and "no search results" mysteries on device.
- **2026-06-06** — Polish batch: NearMeUserSheet self-loads its data (follow state, recents) and adds an inline follow button + multi-track list (3 spotlights + 5 recents). UserProfileView's spotlight section is now a read-only `SpotlightCarousel` with historic browsing — mirrors own-profile, no remove button. Confirmed Shelf's chronological-by-release-date album sort within artist was already implemented in `groupByAlbum`.
- **2026-06-06** — Created this CLAUDE.md after the Search overhaul + Feed simplification. Drift-prevention.
- **2026-06-06** — Dropped "Your Music" toggle from Feed; feed is Following-only. User's own recents live on Profile.
- **2026-06-06** — Search overhaul: People/Music mode toggle, Music sub-scope (All/Artists/Albums/Songs), artists and albums rendered as horizontal carousels, songs as vertical list.
- **2026-06-06** — Cleaned up models: deleted dead `SpotifyTrack` decoder + `Song.fromSpotify` (~30 LOC), made `Song.sourceService` non-optional defaulting to `.appleMusic`, renamed Firestore catalog collection from `catalog` → `tracks` to match ERD, flattened `CatalogTrack.ServiceMap` → top-level `appleMusicUri` / `appleMusicTrackId` fields.
- **2026-06-06** — Phase A2 complete: preview playback via `AVPlayer` + `Song.previewAssets` (the right MusicKit API; previous attempt used the wrong property name `previews`). Non-subscribers get 30-second previews in-app; `trackUnavailable` fires only if both full + preview paths fail.
- **2026-06-06** — Phase A1 complete: `MusicSubscription` observation on `AppleMusicService`, two-row ProfileView music section (auth + subscription), system `.musicSubscriptionOffer(...)` for in-app sign-up, `PlaybackErrorAlert` view modifier on MiniPlayerBar + NowPlayingView.

- **2026-06-06** — Spotify→Apple Music port complete. Deleted `API/Spotify/`, `SpotifyAuthViewModel`, `SpotifyLinkView`, `LinkedServices.swift`. Created `AppleMusicService` conforming to `MusicService`. Refactored every consumer (no more token threading at play call sites — `playerVM.play(.from(post: post))` etc.). Build clean.
- **2026-06-06** — SwiftUI preview wiring landed: `PreviewFixtures` + `mockPreview` factories on every VM + `View.withPreviewEnvironment()` extension. Every screen previewable without simulator.
- **2026-06-06** — Doc-comment headers added to all screens / view models / key services. Repo navigable by reading file headers.

---

## 11. When in doubt

- Ask the user before making a product call. The vision lives in their head; INTENDED_BEHAVIOUR.md in the RN repo is older but useful for cross-reference.
- The user has been the source of truth on every product decision so far. Don't invent product direction.
- For pure engineering choices (file structure, naming, refactor approach), use judgment — the user trusts execution.
